#+TITLE: OpenCPMD rank and thread evidence
#+OPTIONS: toc:nil todo:nil num:nil author:nil

Evidence only, not for upstream as is.
The numbers below are measurements of OpenCPMD
~062582b7cfd832d36f88f504cd08e4ead42eb404~.
They are not a statement about cpmdc.

* What is measured
Stock ~cpmd.x~ and seven variants of the same tree, on two cells, at
MPI ranks 1, 2, 3, 4 and OpenMP threads 1, 2, 8.
Five repeats per cell, interleaved by repeat, then system, then rank,
then thread, then variant.
A separate strong-scaling slice uses 1 thread and ranks 1, 2, 4, 8.

Each SCF uses a fresh directory, so RESTART is not reused.
~CONVERGENCE ORBITALS~ is ~1.0d-12~.
Si8 ~MAXSTEP~ is 20.
Water ~MAXSTEP~ is 30.
Both cells hit the step cap.
On stock at 1 rank and 1 thread, Si8 ends step 20 at ~gemax~ 4.169e-8,
still falling, so a 1e-8 threshold is not reached.
Water first prints ~gemax~ below 1e-8 at step 24 and stays between
5e-9 and 1e-8 through step 30.
A threshold stop can still land on different steps when the gradient
history differs, so the iteration count is the cap.

* Build
Compiler: GNU Fortran 13.3.0.
MPI: Open MPI 4.1.6.
BLAS: OpenBLAS 0.3.26, ~OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1~.
Stock FFT: Goedecker (~-D__HAS_FFT_DEFAULT~).
FFTW variant: FFTW 3.3.10, ~-D__HAS_FFT_FFTW3~, ~-lfftw3 -lfftw3_omp~.
FFTW is entered with ~use_wisdom=.FALSE.~ at
~fftmain_utils.mod.F90:362~, and ~mltfft_utils.mod.F90:504-522~ maps
that to ~FFTW_ESTIMATE~ (flag 64).
~utils.mod.F90:185-187~ calls ~DFFTW_INIT_THREADS~ and
~DFFTW_PLAN_WITH_NTHREADS~ when OpenMP and FFTW3 are both on.
The evidence macro forces ESTIMATE as well.

Flags, from ~configure/LINUX-X86_64-GFORTRAN-MPI-EVIDENCE~, with ~-omp~:

#+begin_example
FC=mpif90
FFLAGS=-O2 -Wall -fPIC -ffp-contract=off -ffree-line-length-none -fopenmp -fallow-argument-mismatch
CPPFLAGS=-D__Linux -D__GNU -D__PARALLEL -D__HAS_FFT_DEFAULT
LFLAGS=-lopenblas
#+end_example

The FFTW config is the same with ~__HAS_FFT_FFTW3~ and
~-lopenblas -lfftw3 -lfftw3_omp~.
~-ffp-contract=off~ is on for every variant.

#+begin_src bash
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh stock
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh a_gather
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh a_exact
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh b_gorder
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh d_omp
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh ab
bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh full
OPENCPMD_WORK=/tmp/opencpmd-work-fftw OPENCPMD_DEST=/tmp/opencpmd-build-fftw \
  bench/opencpmd_invariance/build_opencpmd.sh fftw
#+end_src

The OpenCPMD tree must already be at
~062582b7cfd832d36f88f504cd08e4ead42eb404~ in ~$OPENCPMD_SRC~
(default /tmp/opencpmd-src).
~patches/evidence.patch~ is the instrumentation.
It is evidence only, not for upstream as is.
Behavior is selected by ~src/evidence_flags.h~.
Absent macros keep the stock operation.

* Machine and pinning
The runs in this directory were taken on one VM with 8 cores
(Intel Xeon, KVM, AVX-512).
~OMP_PROC_BIND=close~, ~OMP_PLACES=cores~, ~OMP_WAIT_POLICY=PASSIVE~.
If ~ranks * threads~ is at most 8, ~mpirun --bind-to core --map-by slot:PE=$threads~.
If it is larger, ~mpirun --bind-to none --oversubscribe~.
Rank 4 with 8 threads is 32-way oversubscription.
Those cells are still used for the bitwise comparison.
Their wall times are not a scaling measurement.

* Inputs
~inputs/si8.inp~: 8-atom diamond, LDA (Goedecker-Hutter-Teter Pade),
25 Ry, gamma, cell 10.26 Bohr, ~Si_SGS_LDA.psp~ (BHS, LMAX=P).
~inputs/water.inp~: one water, BLYP, 70 Ry, periodic 10 Angstrom cube,
~O_GTH_BLYP.psp~ and ~H_GTH_BLYP.psp~.
Both request ~PRINT FORCES ON~ and ~STRESS TENSOR~.
~INITIALIZE WAVEFUNCTION ATOMS~.
Pseudopotentials are under ~pp/~.

* Variants
- stock: MPI_SUM and the original OpenMP reductions. Goedecker FFT.
- a_gather: ~mp_sum~ real and complex arrays are gathered to rank 0 in
  rank order, added in that order, and broadcast.
  ~src/mp_interface.mod.F90:1027-1030~ includes the four sum files.
- a_exact: the same ~mp_sum~ values are packed as ~x * 2^90~ into int128
  and reduced with a local ~MPI_Op~.
  Bits that do not fit, including subnormals, are summed afterwards in
  rank order as an f64 residual and added back.
  A one-rank call therefore returns the input bits.
  Zeroing those residuals instead of summing them moved the Si8 energy
  by about 0.28 Ha at initialization, because values near 1e-30 were
  flushed to zero.
- b_gorder: kinetic energy, when ~akin~ is below the existing threshold,
  sums per-G terms in a packed global G key order
  (~kin_energy_utils.mod.F90:64-110~).
  ~dotp~ / ~dotp_c~ (~dotp_utils.mod.F90:51~ and ~:96~) use a sequential
  sum instead of ~ddot~.
  This is a local reassociation. It does not by itself make two rank
  counts bitwise equal, because the orbital coefficients still depend
  on the plane distribution.
- d_omp: OpenMP ~REDUCTION~ loops in the density, energy, force, and
  stress files are replaced by chunks of 64, summed in chunk-index
  order. The files and the number of loops are in
  ~patches/omp_rewrite.log~.
  Upstream sites include ~rhoofr_utils.mod.F90:551~ and ~:612~,
  ~stress_utils.mod.F90:421~, ~:438~, ~:454~,
  ~vlocst_utils.mod.F90:72~, ~:88~, ~:112~,
  ~potfor_utils.mod.F90:128~ and ~:168~.
  ~loadpa_utils.mod.F90:441-488~ (~iraymax~) keeps the last maximum with
  ~>=~. Under the OpenMP flag that scan is serial, in the same index
  order as one thread, so the owned G ray does not depend on the thread
  count.
  Loops left on the stock OpenMP reduction include
  ~vlocst_utils.mod.F90:105~ (bare ~TEMP~ array),
  ~htrstr_utils.mod.F90~ unbalanced region logged at the patched line 455,
  ~pi_stress_utils.mod.F90:71~, ~:107~, ~:183~, ~:203~, ~:264~, ~:288~,
  ~rhoofr_kdp_utils.mod.F90:316~, ~rhoofr_p_utils.mod.F90:252~,
  and the integer ray counts in ~loadpa_utils.mod.F90:190~ and ~:222~.
  ~nlforce_utils.mod.F90~ and ~rnlfor_utils.mod.F90~ had no plus-reduction
  the rewriter could take.
- ab: gather plus G order.
- full: gather plus G order plus the fixed OpenMP chunks.
- fftw: stock reductions with FFTW ESTIMATE.

The plane split itself stays rank-dependent.
~loadpa_utils.mod.F90:145-160~ sets ~nrxpl~ from ~xplanes/nproc~ and
~NINT~. An identical global FFT plan would require replicating the mesh.
The driver records ~ngw~ and ~nrxpl~ per rank. The 1D FFT length is the
global mesh.

* Dump and CSV
~CPMDC_EVIDENCE_BIN~ selects a stream file written on the I/O parent at
the end of ~OPTIMIZE WAVEFUNCTION~, from ~rwfopt_utils~ just before
~finalp~.
~fion~ is the nuclear gradient. The file stores the physical force,
~-fion~.
Stress is ~paiu~, column major, before the kBar print.
GPa is ~(paiu/omega)*au_kb*0.1~ with ~au_kb=294210.1080~ from
~cnst.mod.F90~.
~CPMDC_EVIDENCE_STEPS~ logs each SCF step's ~tcpu~.
In the normal loop (~rwfopt_utils.mod.F90:496-536~) ~time1~ is reset
every iteration, so each sample is that step in seconds.
The printed TCPU column is ~0PF10.2~, which is coarser than a Si8 step.
~wall_step_s~ is the median of those full-precision samples.
The last step also writes the restart file.

CSV columns:
~system,ranks,threads,rep,quantity,index,value_hex,value,iters,wall_step_s,variant~.
~value_hex~ is the IEEE-754 word, big-endian.
One shard per system and variant, under ~results/~.
Run directories under ~results/runs/~ are local and are not part of the
tree.

#+begin_src bash
python3 bench/opencpmd_invariance/run_grid.py \
  --variants stock,a_gather,a_exact,b_gorder,d_omp,ab,full,fftw --reps 5
python3 bench/opencpmd_invariance/run_grid.py \
  --variants stock,full --ranks 8 --threads 1 --reps 5
python3 bench/opencpmd_invariance/analyze.py
#+end_src

* Tables
~analyze.py~ writes ~results/tables.org~ from the CSV shards.
The same four tables are copied below.
960 runs cover ranks 1, 2, 3, 4 and threads 1, 2, 8, five repeats,
eight variants, two cells.
Stock and ~full~ add ranks 8 at 1 thread, five repeats, both cells.
Every run stopped at the step cap: Si8 at 20, water at 30.
No run exited nonzero.

Bitwise equality across the whole grid holds for unused fields that
stay zero, for ~eself~ on both cells, and for ~esr~ on water.
It does not hold for ~etot~, the SCF energy terms, the forces, or the
stress, on stock or on any variant.
Rank groups at 1 thread stay at 4 for forces and stress on every
variant, including ~a_gather~, ~a_exact~, ~b_gorder~, ~d_omp~, ~ab~,
and ~full~.
Rank 8 is a further distinct hex from rank 1 (~r8 with r1~ is 2) for
~etot~ and forces on stock and ~full~.
The plane split in ~loadpa_utils.mod.F90:145-160~ is still rank
dependent, and these reductions do not remove that.

~d_omp~ is the switch that changes run-to-run force scatter.
Stock forces share one hex in 10 of 13 Si8 cells and 9 of 13 water
cells.
~d_omp~ forces share one hex in all 12 cells of the rank 1-4 grid.
~full~ forces share one hex in all 13 cells, including rank 8.
Thread groups at rank 1 for those forces stay at 3: one hex per thread
count.
Stock water forces have 5 thread groups at rank 1, so repeats at one
thread count do not all match.
The largest force deviation from the same variant's 1x1 repeat 0 is
4.394e-8 Ha/Bohr on Si8 stock and on Si8 ~d_omp~, 1.146e-8 Ha/Bohr on
water stock, and 5.381e-9 Ha/Bohr on water ~d_omp~.
~etot~ deviations stay at or below 6.253e-13 Ha on water stock and
4.619e-14 Ha on Si8 stock.
Stress deviations stay at or below 3.374e-6 GPa on Si8 stock and
2.245e-7 GPa on water stock.

On water at 1 thread the median step is long enough to compare.
Stock medians are 0.4402, 0.2228, 0.1504, 0.1098, and 0.0635 s at
ranks 1, 2, 3, 4, and 8.
~full~ medians at those cells are 0.8%, -0.6%, 1.3%, 3.0%, and 6.8%
from stock.
The 8-rank ranges are 0.0608-0.0689 s (stock) and 0.0656-0.0680 s
(~full~).
~a_exact~ at water 1x1 has median 0.4593 s against stock 0.4402 s.
The ranges overlap (0.4280-0.4878 s and 0.4272-0.4968 s).
FFTW ESTIMATE at water 1x1 has median 0.3666 s, range 0.3516-0.3923 s,
which does not overlap stock.
Si8 steps are 0.0114 s at 1 rank and 0.0028 s at 8 ranks.
Percent overheads there sit inside the repeat range, except as a
median shift.
Several 2-thread water cells are bimodal, with clusters near 0.08 s
and 0.12 s in stock and in the variants.
A median taken from five such samples can move by about half without
a separate algorithm cost.
~oversub=yes~ cells are the ones with ~ranks * threads~ above 8.
They are kept for the bitwise comparison and left out of the scaling
reading.

The gather path replaces one tree allreduce with a gather to rank 0,
a sum in rank order, and a broadcast.
That extra traffic grows with the reduced length and with the rank
count.
There is no Si64 point in this harness.
The water 8-rank cell is where the median step of ~full~ already sits
6.8% above stock.

Strong scaling uses stock and ~full~ at 1 thread.
Parallel efficiency is the 1-rank median divided by ~ranks~ times the
median at that rank.
Si8 stock falls to 0.518 at 8 ranks because the step is about 11 ms.
Water stock is 0.866 at 8 ranks, and ~full~ is 0.817.
The limiter on Si8 is the step duration against MPI latency.
On water the step is long enough that efficiency stays above 0.8, and
the gather is visible as the gap between stock and ~full~ at 8 ranks.
An embed single point through ~libcpmdc~ was not repeated.

* How a discrepancy is attributed
Compare one variant at a time with stock, then the cumulative sets.
A quantity is bitwise equal across the grid when every rank, thread,
and repeat carries one hex for every component.
The bitwise table counts, in each cell, how many repeats match the
same variant's 1-rank 1-thread repeat 0, and how many repeats share
the largest hex. The second count is run-to-run agreement.
The first count mixes that with decomposition scatter.
The attribution table splits the same hexes three ways.
~cells r2r~ is how many cells have one hex across their repeats.
~thread groups at r1~ counts distinct hexes across threads 1, 2, and 8
at 1 rank, repeats included.
~rank groups at t1~ counts distinct hexes across ranks 1, 2, 3, and 4
at 1 thread, repeats included.
~r8 with r1~ counts distinct hexes among rank 1 and rank 8 at 1 thread.
It is ~na~ for variants that were not run at rank 8.
One group on an axis means that axis does not change the bits.
Stock and ~full~ have 65 runs (13 cells) because they include that
rank-8 slice. The other variants have 60 runs (12 cells).
~a_gather~ changes the MPI sum order only.
~d_omp~ and the serial ~iraymax~ change the thread order.
~b_gorder~ changes the local G sum order.
~fftw~ changes the FFT backend, not the plane split.
~a_exact~ is the int128 reduction plus the rank-ordered residual.
The cost table is the median and the range of ~wall_step_s~, and the
overhead against stock at the same cell.
~oversub~ is yes when ~ranks * threads~ exceeds 8, the core count of
the machine that produced these numbers.
Those cells stay in the bitwise table.
Their wall times are not a scaling measurement.
